[Dependency] private SharedDoorSystem _doorSystem = default!;\n    [Dependency] private IGameTiming _gameTiming = default!;\n\n    public override void Initialize()\n    {\n ComponentStartup>(OnComponentStartup);\n\n        SubscribeLocalEvent<FirelockComponent, ExaminedEvent>(OnExamined);\n    }\n\n    public bool EmergencyPressureStop(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent? firelock = null, DoorComponent? door = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref firelock, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        if (door.State != DoorState.Open\n            || firelock.EmergencyCloseCooldown != null\n            && _gameTiming.CurTime < firelock.EmergencyCloseCooldown)\n            return false;\n\n        if (!_doorSystem.TryClose(uid, door))\n            return false;\n\n        return _doorSystem.OnPartialClose(uid, door);\n    }\n\n    #region Access/Prying\n\n    private void OnBeforeDoorOpened(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent component, BeforeDoorOpenedEvent args)\n    {\n        // Give the Door remote the ability to force a firelock open even if it is holding back dangerous gas\n        var overrideAccess = (args.User != null) && _accessReaderSystem.IsAllowed(args.User.Value, uid);\n\n        if (!component.Powered || (!overrideAccess && component.IsLocked))\n            args.Cancel();\n        else if (args.User != null)\n            WarnPlayer((uid, component), args.User.Value);\n    }\n\n    private void OnBeforePry(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent component, ref BeforePryEvent args)\n    {\n        if (args.Cancelled || !component.Powered || args.StrongPry || args.PryPowered)\n            return;\n\n        args.Cancelled = true;\n    }\n\n    private void OnDoorGetPryTimeModifier(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent component, ref GetPryTimeModifierEvent args)\n    {\n        WarnPlayer((uid, component), args.User);\n\n        if (component.IsLocked)\n            args.PryTimeModifier *= component.LockedPryTimeModifier;\n    }\n\n    private void WarnPlayer(Entity<FirelockComponent> ent, EntityUid user)\n    {\n        if (ent.Comp.Temperature)\n        {\n            _popupSystem.PopupEntity(Loc.GetString(\"firelock-component-is-holding-fire-message\"),\n                ent.Owner,\n                user,\n                PopupType.MediumCaution);\n        }\n        else if (ent.Comp.Pressure)\n        {\n            _popupSystem.PopupEntity(Loc.GetString(\"firelock-component-is-holding-pressure-message\"),\n                ent.Owner,\n                user,\n                PopupType.MediumCaution);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void OnAfterPried(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent component, ref PriedEvent args)\n    {\n        component.EmergencyCloseCooldown = _gameTiming.CurTime + component.EmergencyCloseCooldownDuration;\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Visuals\n\n    protected virtual void OnComponentStartup(Entity<FirelockComponent> ent, ref ComponentStartup args)\n    {\n        UpdateVisuals(ent.Owner,ent.Comp, args);\n    }\n\n    private void UpdateVisuals(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent component, EntityEventArgs args) => UpdateVisuals(uid, component);\n\n    private void UpdateVisuals(EntityUid uid,\n        FirelockComponent? firelock = null,\n        DoorComponent? door = null,\n        AppearanceComponent? appearance = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door, ref appearance, false))\n            return;\n\n        // only bother to check pressure on doors that are some variation of closed.\n        if (door.State != DoorState.Closed\n            && door.State != DoorState.Welded\n            && door.State != DoorState.Denying)\n        {\n            _appearance.SetData(uid, DoorVisuals.ClosedLights, false, appearance);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref firelock, ref appearance, false))\n            return;\n\n        _appearance.SetData(uid, DoorVisuals.ClosedLights, firelock.IsLocked, appearance);\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    private void OnExamined(Entity<FirelockComponent> ent, ref ExaminedEvent args)\n    {\n        using (args.PushGroup(nameof(FirelockComponent)))\n        {\n            if (ent.Comp.Pressure)\n                args.PushMarkup(Loc.GetString(\"firelock-component-examine-pressure-warning\"));\n            if (ent.Comp.Temperature)\n                args.PushMarkup(Loc.GetString(\"firelock-component-examine-temperature-warning\"));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n[Serializable, NetSerializable]\npublic enum FirelockVisuals : byte\n{\n FirelockVisualLayersTemperature : byte\n{\n    Base\n}\nusing Content.Shared.Guidebook;\nusing Robust.Shared.GameStates;\n\nnamespace Content.Shared.Doors.Components;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Companion component to <see cref=\"DoorComponent\"/> that handles firelock-specific behavior, including\n/// auto-closing on depressurization, air/fire alarm interactions, and preventing normal door functions when\n/// retaining pressure..\n/// </summary>\n[RegisterComponent, NetworkedComponent, AutoGenerateComponentState]\npublic sealed partial class FirelockComponent : Component\n{\n    #region Settings\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Pry time modifier to be used when the firelock is currently closed due to fire or pressure.\n    /// </summary>\n    [DataField]\n    public float LockedPryTimeModifier = 1.5f;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Maximum pressure difference before the firelock will refuse to open, in kPa.\n    /// </summary>\n    [DataField, GuidebookData]\n    public float PressureThreshold = 20;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Maximum temperature difference before the firelock will refuse to open, in k.\n    /// </summary>\n    [DataField, GuidebookData]\n    public float TemperatureThreshold = 230;\n    // this used to check for hot-spots, but because accessing that data is a a mess this now just checks\n    // temperature.     private const int UpdateInterval = 30;\n        private int _accumulatedTicks;\n\n        public override void Initialize()\n        {\n            base.Initialize();\n\n            SubscribeLocalEvent<FirelockComponent, AtmosAlarmEvent>(OnAtmosAlarm);\n            SubscribeLocalEvent<FirelockComponent, PowerChangedEvent>(PowerChanged);\n        }\n\n        private void public override void Update(float frameTime)\n        {\n            _accumulatedTicks += 1;\n            if (_accumulatedTicks < UpdateInterval)\n                return;\n\n            _accumulatedTicks = 0;\n\n\n            var query = EntityQueryEnumerator<FirelockComponent, DoorComponent>();\n            while (query.MoveNext(out var uid, out var firelock, out var door))\n            {\n                if (_atmosAlarmQuery.TryComp(uid, out var alarmable)\n                    && alarmable.LastAlarmState == AtmosAlarmType.Danger\n                    && this.IsPowered(uid, EntityManager)\n                    && door.State == DoorState.Open)\n                {\n                    EmergencyPressureStop(uid, firelock, door);\n                }\n\n                // only bother to check pressure on doors that are some variation of closed.\n                if (door.State != DoorState.Closed\n                    && door.State != DoorState.Welded\n                    && && _appearanceQuery.TryGetComponent(uid, out var appearance))\n                {\n                    var (pressure, fire) = CheckPressureAndFire(uid, firelock, airtight);\n    _appearance.SetData(uid, FirelockVisuals.PressureWarning, pressure, appearance);\n                    _appearance.SetData(uid, FirelockVisuals.TemperatureWarning, fire, appearance);\n                    Dirty(uid, firelock);\n\n                    if (_pointLightQuery.TryComp(uid, out var pointLight))\n                    {\n                        _pointLight.SetEnabled(uid, fire | pressure,        if (!this.IsPowered(uid, EntityManager))\n                return;\n\n            if (!TryComp<DoorComponent>(uid, out var doorComponent))\n                return;\n\n            if (args.AlarmType == AtmosAlarmType.Normal)\n            {\n                if (doorComponent.State == DoorState.Closed)\n                    _doorSystem.TryOpen(uid);\n            }\n            else if (args.AlarmType == AtmosAlarmType.Danger)\n            {\n                EmergencyPressureStop(uid, component, doorComponent);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public (bool Pressure, bool CheckPressureAndFire(uid, firelock, airtight);\n            return (false, false);\n        }\n\n        public (bool Pressure, bool Fire) CheckPressureAndFire(\n        EntityUid uid,\n        FirelockComponent firelock,\n        AirtightComponent airtight)\n        {\n            if (!airtight.AirBlocked)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            if (TryComp(uid, out DockingComponent? dock) && dock.Docked)\n            {\n                // Currently docking automatically opens the doors. But maybe in future, check the pressure difference before opening doors?\n                return (false, false);\n            }\n\n            var xform = Transform(uid);\n            if (!HasComp<GridAtmosphereComponent>(xform.ParentUid))\n                return (false, false);\n\n            var grid = Comp<MapGridComponent>(xform.ParentUid);\n            var pos = _mapping.CoordinatesToTile(xform.ParentUid, grid, xform.Coordinates);\n            var minPressure = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxPressure = float.MinValue;\n            var minTemperature = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxTemperature = float.MinValue;\n            var holdingFire = false;\n            var holdingPressure = false;\n\n            // We cannot simply use `_atmosSystem.GetAdjacentTileMixtures` because of how the `includeBlocked` option\n            // works, we want to ignore the firelock's blocking, while including blockers on other tiles.\n            // GetAdjacentTileMixtures also ignores empty/non-existent tiles, which we don't want. Additionally, for\n            // edge-fire locks, we only want to enumerate over a single directions. So AFAIK there is no nice way of\n            // achieving all this using existing atmos functions, and the functionality is too specialized to bother\n            // adding new public atmos system functions.\n\n            List<Vector2i> tiles = new(4);\n            List<AtmosDirection> directions = new(4);\n            for (var i = 0; i < Atmospherics.Directions; i++)\n            {\n                var dir = (AtmosDirection)(1 << i);\n                if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection.HasFlag(dir))\n                {\n                    directions.Add(dir);\n                    tiles.Add(pos.Offset(dir));\n                }\n            }\n\n            // May also have to consider pressure on the same tile as the firelock.\n            var count = tiles.Count;\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection != AtmosDirection.All)\n                tiles.Add(pos);\n\n            var gasses = _atmosSystem.GetTileMixtures(xform.ParentUid, xform.MapUid, tiles);\n            if (gasses == null)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)\n            {\n                var gas = gasses[i];\n                var dir = directions[i];\n                var adjacentPos = tiles[i];\n\n                if (gas != null)\n                {\n                    // Is there some airtight entity blocking this direction? If yes, don't include this direction in the\n                    // pressure differential\n                    if (HasAirtightBlocker(_mapping.GetAnchoredEntities(xform.ParentUid, grid, adjacentPos), dir.GetOpposite()))\n                        continue;\n\n                    var p = gas.Pressure;\n                    minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                    maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                    minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                    maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                }\n\n                holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n                holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n                if (holdingPressure && holdingFire)\n                    return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n            }\n\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection == AtmosDirection.All)\n                return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n\n            var local = gasses[count];\n            if (local != null)\n            {\n                var p = local.Pressure;\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, local.Temperature);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, local.Temperature);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, 0);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, 0);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, 0);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, 0);\n            }\n\n            holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n            holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n            return (holdingPressure,     }\n\n        public (bool Pressure, bool Fire) CheckPressureAndFire(\n        EntityUid uid,\n        FirelockComponent firelock,\n        AirtightComponent airtight)\n        {\n            if (!airtight.AirBlocked)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            if (TryComp(uid, out DockingComponent? dock) && dock.Docked)\n            {\n                // Currently docking automatically opens the doors. But maybe in future, check the pressure difference before opening doors?\n                return (false, false);\n            }\n\n            var xform = Transform(uid);\n            if (!HasComp<GridAtmosphereComponent>(xform.ParentUid))\n                return (false, false);\n\n            var grid = Comp<MapGridComponent>(xform.ParentUid);\n            var pos = _mapping.CoordinatesToTile(xform.ParentUid, grid, xform.Coordinates);\n            var minPressure = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxPressure = float.MinValue;\n            var minTemperature = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxTemperature = float.MinValue;\n            var holdingFire = false;\n            var holdingPressure = false;\n\n            // We cannot simply use `_atmosSystem.GetAdjacentTileMixtures` because of how the `includeBlocked` option\n            // works, we want to ignore the firelock's blocking, while including blockers on other tiles.\n            // GetAdjacentTileMixtures also ignores empty/non-existent tiles, which we don't want. Additionally, for\n            // edge-fire locks, we only want to enumerate over a single directions. So AFAIK there is no nice way of\n            // achieving all this using existing atmos functions, and the functionality is too specialized to bother\n            // adding new public atmos system functions.\n\n            List<Vector2i> tiles = new(4);\n            List<AtmosDirection> directions = new(4);\n            for (var i = 0; i < Atmospherics.Directions; i++)\n            {\n                var dir = (AtmosDirection)(1 << i);\n                if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection.HasFlag(dir))\n                {\n                    directions.Add(dir);\n                    tiles.Add(pos.Offset(dir));\n                }\n            }\n\n            // May also have to consider pressure on the same tile as the firelock.\n            var count = tiles.Count;\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection != AtmosDirection.All)\n                tiles.Add(pos);\n\n            var gasses = _atmosSystem.GetTileMixtures(xform.ParentUid, xform.MapUid, tiles);\n            if (gasses == null)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)\n            {\n                var gas = gasses[i];\n                var dir = directions[i];\n                var adjacentPos = tiles[i];\n\n                if (gas != null)\n                {\n                    // Is there some airtight entity blocking this direction? If yes, don't include this direction in the\n                    // pressure differential\n                    if (HasAirtightBlocker(_mapping.GetAnchoredEntities(xform.ParentUid, grid, adjacentPos), dir.GetOpposite()))\n                        continue;\n\n                    var p = gas.Pressure;\n                    minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                    maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                    minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                    maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                }\n\n                holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n                holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n                if (holdingPressure && holdingFire)\n                    return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n            }\n\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection == AtmosDirection.All)\n                return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n\n            var local = gasses[count];\n            if (local != null)\n            {\n                var p = local.Pressure;\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, local.Temperature);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, local.Temperature);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, 0);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, 0);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, 0);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, 0);\n            }\n\n            holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n            holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n            return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n        }\n\n        private bool HasAirtightBlocker(IEnumerable<EntityUid> enumerable, AtmosDirection dir)\n        {\n            foreach (var ent in enumerable)\n            {\n                if (!_airtightQuery.TryGetComponent(ent, out var airtight) || !airtight.AirBlocked)\n                    continue;\n\n                if ((airtight.AirBlockedDirection & dir) == dir)\n                    return true;\n            }\n\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n}\n- type: entity\n  abstract: true\n  parent: [ BaseStructure, StructureHealthDoor ]\n  id: BaseFirelock\n  name: firelock\n  description: Apply crowbar.\n  components:\n  - OnHandleState(Entity<DoorComponent> ent, ref AfterAutoHandleStateEvent args)\n    {\n        var door = ent.Comp;\n        if (door.NextStateChange == null)\n            _activeDoors.Remove(ent);\n door = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        // If no change, return to avoid firing a new DoorStateChangedEvent.\n        if (state == door.State)\n GameTiming.CurTime + door.CloseTimeOne;\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Denying:\n                _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n                door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.DenyDuration;\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Emagging:\n                _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n                door.NextStateChange = door = null, EntityUid? user = null, bool partial = false)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        // since both closing/closed and welded are door states, we need to prevent 'closing'\n        // a welded door or else there will be weird state bugs\n        if (door.State is DoorState.Welded or DoorState.Closed)\n            return false;\n\n        if (Paused(uid))\n            return false;\n\n        var ev = new BeforeDoorClosedEvent(door.PerformCollisionCheck, partial);\n        RaiseLocalEvent(uid, ev);\n        if (ev.Cancelled)\n            return false;\n\n        if (!HasAccess(uid, user, door))\n            return false;\n\n        return !ev.PerformCollisionCheck || !GetColliding(uid, null, null, door.CheckFixtureCollision, door.PerformCollisionCheck).Any();\n    }\n\n    public void StartClosing(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? Otherwise, returns true;\n    /// </summary>\n    public bool OnPartialClose(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? door = null, PhysicsComponent? physics = null)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door, ref physics))\n            return false;\n\n        // Make sure no entity walked into the airlock when it started closing.\n        if (!CanClose(uid, door, partial: true))\n        {\n            door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.OpenTimeTwo;\n            door.State = DoorState.Open;\n            AppearanceSystem.SetData(uid, DoorVisuals.State, DoorState.Open);\n            Dirty(uid, door);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        door.Partial = true;\n        SetCollidable(uid, true, door, physics);\n        door.NextStateChange = GameTiming.CurTime + door.CloseTimeTwo;\n        Dirty(uid, door);\n        _activeDoors.Add((uid, door));\n\n        // Crush any entities. Note that we don't check airlock safety here. Note that we don't check \"can open\"\n            // here. The door never actually finished closing and we don't want people to get stuck inside of doors.\n            StartOpening(ent, door);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        switch (door.State)\n        {\n            case DoorState.Opening:\n                // Either fully or partially open this door.\n                if (door.Partial)\n                    SetState(ent, DoorState.Open, door);\n                else\n                    OnPartialOpen(ent, door);\n\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Closing:\n                // Either fully or partially close this door.\n                if (door.Partial)\n                    SetState(ent, DoorState.Closed, door);\n                else\n                    OnPartialClose(ent, door);\n\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Denying:\n                // Finish denying entry and return to the closed state.\n                SetState(ent, DoorState.Closed, door);\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Emagging:\n                StartOpening(ent, door);\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Open:\n                // This door is open, and queued for an auto-close.\n                if (!TryClose(ent, door))\n                {\n                    // The door failed to close (blocked?). Try again in one second.\n                    door.NextStateChange = time + TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1);\n                }\n                break;\n\n            case DoorState.Welded:\n                // A welded door? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_vrtx_011ChDPadjT3SjQiJW4gqpJY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I couldn't watch the clip: `/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty and there's no `recording.mp4` anywhere on the machine. So I worked from the expected behaviour you listed and checked the firelock code, prototypes and sprite files for the kinds of problems you described. I fixed five. The server, shared and client code all build, and the 31 airtight and delta-pressure integration tests pass. I didn't run the game to look at the doors, so none of this has been checked against what the clip shows.\n\n**What ends up on each side of a shut door**\n1. **Gas leaked through shut doors** (`Content.Server/Atmos/EntitySystems/AirtightSystem.cs`). When a door opened, the game told the air simulation the tile was open again. When it closed, that update was skipped, so the simulation kept treating the tile as open and gas kept flowing. It now updates the tile on both opening and closing.\n2. **Edge firelocks sealed the wrong side** (`firelock.yml`). The sprite and the collision box sit on the south edge of the tile, but the air seal was on the north edge. It's now `South`, the same as windoors.\n\n**Warning lights**\n3. **Warnings stayed on after the door opened** (`SharedFirelockSystem.cs`). There was a handler meant to run when the door changes state, but it was never hooked up. It now is. While the door isn't shut, the pressure and temperature lights and the red point light are turned off. While it's shut, they match the danger it's holding back.\n   - I also reset the stored pressure/temperature danger flags when the door opens. Without this, a door could flash an old warning for up to half a second after closing again. One side effect: examining an open firelock no longer shows a warning.\n4. **Glass firelocks couldn't say what kind of danger it was** (`firelock.yml`). Their sprite had no pressure or temperature warning layers. I added them. I also confirmed the colours aren't swapped: pressure is cyan and temperature is orange, matching the examine text.\n\n**Door movement and alignment**\n5. **The standard firelock snapped shut** (`Standard/firelock-base.rsi/meta.json`). Its closing animation timings were 10× too short: 0.05s in total against 0.5s for opening and for the glass version. Because of that, the door panel finished long before its light overlay, which played at normal speed, so the two drifted apart. I set the closing timings to match opening, so the panel and lights move together again.\n\nAs a check on the sprite sheets, every closing animation is exactly its opening animation played backwards, and the frame sizes and positions are consistent.
